## 2. Seven-dimension comparison

### 1. Argument and structure
**Reader question:** What does the single 68.4% scored-case pass rate compress, and what do the environment map + tuple matrix add (and still cannot establish)?

**Strongest source-supported thesis:** On this archived seed-0 selection of 416 cases (405 scored), Mercury-2.5 received 277 PASS / 128 FAIL (68.4%). The distribution is endpoint-heavy (17 all-pass 172/172, 7 scored zero-pass 0/68, 12 mixed 105/165 +1 unscored, 2 fully unscored). The 340-row environment×tuple matrix is mostly singleton/binary (296/340 single-case; 320/329 rated rows exactly 0 or 1; 9 fractionals all in `moco`); dimensions are environment-specific (no shared scale). Selection (80 omissions), single seed, dirty source, guarantee/environment confound, undefined dispositions, and open 14-vs-24 / 7-vs-10 discrepancies bound every causal or general-capability reading.

**Structures:**
- `gpt 6 luna max`: Cleaner linear count → endpoints → names-as-handles → matrix → withdrawn/open → unscored → 7-vs-10 → labels/guarantees → selection → confounds → supports/does-not. Slightly more compressed; ends with broken “!” + Figure 2.
- `opus 5`: Similar skeleton with stronger density/sensitivity arithmetic early, more explicit “what this does not show,” and a live Fig 2 embed. Slightly more rhetorical framing of the pooled number as “descriptively thin.”

**Move/combine/shorten/remove:**
- Progress: intelligible pooled-rate problem → exact counts + selection frame (Fig 8) → endpoint map (Fig 9) → density/names caution → tuple matrix (Fig 10) → failure labels (Fig 11) + guarantee confound → withdrawn/open + unresolved discrepancies → bounded consequence.
- Avoid audit-findings-as-essay. Shorten repeated caveat lists into point-of-use attachments. Remove any “no breakdown available” claims. Do not sequence every local-check row as prose.

### 2. Opening
**gpt:** Direct 68.4% + ts_* contrast; solid but report-like.
**opus:** “What should I expect if I open one environment at random?” + ts_*; better tension, still close to internal article.

**Proposed fresh strategy (do not draft full opening or copy either):** Open on a concrete, source-supported contrast that makes the pooled rate feel immediately insufficient—e.g., the three `ts_*` environments (4/4 vs 0/4 vs 0/4) or the sheaf split (8/9 vs 0/9 vs 0/9)—then state that 277/405 = 68.4% is the precise answer to one counting question and the rest of the piece maps what that number hides and cannot support. Ground in supplied example; establish single-seed/selected/retrospective limit within first screen. No slogan replacement; no imported reference-post scene.

### 3. Examples and technical depth
**Keep exact supported examples:**
- ts_* trio; sheaf_* split; categorical_lenses 9/9 vs functorial_augmentation 0/9 vs tensor_functor 6/9.
- Density: glyph 0/24, moco 36/55, batchnorm_ema 2/15 → 94/405 cases, 56/128 FAILs.

- rd_adaptive_halting unscored at all-easy five-dimension tuple (missing outcome ≠ easy-case failure).
- 10 rejected FAILs (8 monadic_reward source_invalid, 1 batchnorm_ema, 1 transformer_ssm_lift); 9 scored-FAIL + disposition unscored (all 5 invalid_action, 2 training_failed, 2 lowercase runtime_error).
- ts_parse_only row performance_eligible=False PASS kept in 277; exclusion_reason = recorded provider-attribution conflict.

## 3. Original thesis and opening strategy

**Thesis (source-supported):** The 68.4% (277/405) scored-case pass rate on this archived Mercury-2.5 campaign is arithmetically exact for one question and descriptively thin: environment outcomes are endpoint-heavy, case density is uneven, the 340-row tuple matrix is mostly single-case binary with environment-specific dimensions, and selection, single-seed, dirty-source, guarantee confound, undefined dispositions, and open record discrepancies sharply limit causal, stability, or general-capability claims.

**Reader question:** What does 68.4% hide about where the cases sit, and what can the environment + tuple maps actually support?

**Opening device:** Concrete mini-contrast (ts_* 4/4 vs 0/4 vs 0/4, or equivalent supplied split) that makes “open one environment at random” immediately awkward, then name the precise counting question the 68.4% answers and signal that the piece maps the compression and its bounds. No copied candidate/reference opening; no full draft here.

# Mercury POST-02 — supporting reconciliation of current review claims

Checked 2026-10-29 against the pinned local sources. This note is local row and field verification for the subsequent claim check. It is not an Arena verdict, a replacement review, a prose revision, or final-article validation. The saved candidate and reviewer responses remain unchanged. No browser, Python, or new evaluation run was used.

The review being checked is `Mercury-POST-02-redo-evidence-check-v3.retry-1.responses.md`, labelled `grok 4.5` and `claude fable 5.1`. Its SHA-256 at inspection was `2af1df655f53f29d726b54ec5d8da3e81b3492d6a5997bc0fc5739cf7911ef93`. Citations below use its saved line numbers and section descriptions.

Source identifiers below are relative to `arena-handoffs/Mercury-POST-02-redo-writer-revision-v2.sources/campaign-02-analysis/`, pinned to `6ed48cb1c4447d4f902d1fbc8a908a56185e34ed`:

- **Packet:** `post-production/handoffs/POST-02-redo_evidence_packet.md`, Tables A, B, D.
- **Matrix:** `evidence/generated2/environment_difficulty_matrix.csv`; grouped by `environment`, summing `n_scored` and `n_pass`.
- **Cases:** `evidence/generated/case_results.csv`; grouped by `environment`, `judge_guarantee`, `status`, `verdict`, and `failure_mode_raw` as relevant.
- **Overview:** `ANALYSIS_OVERVIEW.md`, Key patterns 1.
- **Provenance:** `evidence/PROVENANCE.md`, Scope.

PowerShell `Import-Csv`, grouping, and numerical sums were used. The five source hashes were reread and match the corresponding entries in `Mercury-POST-02-editorial-critic-v3.sources.record.json`.

## R1 — nine 9/9 environments; four behavioral-reference and five compile-only

**CORRECTION_REQUIRED in one reviewer sentence.** The matrix contains exactly nine environments with `n_pass = n_scored = 9`. Both candidates' nine-name enumerations are supported, as is the `claude fable 5.1` confirmed-arithmetic row at review line 131. Its later statement at line 176, “four 9/9 environments are behavioral_reference ...; six are compile_only,” has the wrong compile-only count.

| Recorded guarantee | 9/9 environments | Count |
|---|---|---:|
| `behavioral_reference` | `categorical_lenses`, `ci_dependency_graph`, `spreadsheet_dataflow`, `template_interpreter` | 4 |
| `compile_only` | `architecture_naturality`, `neuro_symbolic_parser`, `semiring_unification`, `stochastic_monad`, `tokenizer_adjunction` | 5 |

Source: Packet Table A, the nine named environment rows; Matrix, all rows for those environments; Cases, all selected records for those environments and their `judge_guarantee` fields. Carry **four plus five equals nine** into the next claim check; preserve the original review verbatim.

## R2 — historical “~12 at 1.00” versus 17 all-pass environments

**SUPPORTED historical discrepancy; UNRESOLVED explanation.** The `claude fable 5.1` additions at review lines 164 and 194 are supported. Overview Key patterns 1 says “vs ~12 at 1.00.” Packet Table A and Matrix instead yield 17 environments with positive `n_scored` and no FAILs.

The grouping is nine at 9/9, one at 18/18, two at 15/15, one at 23/23, two at 6/6, and two at 4/4: **17 environments and 172/172 scored cases**. The two environments with no scored outcomes do not enter this count. No rule in the supplied overview explains its approximate twelve. Record this alongside the already identified historical ten versus seven scored zero-pass environments; do not invent a historical filtering rule or replace the source text.

## R3 — provider prose and recorded attempt/time fields

**SUPPORTED field contrast; UNRESOLVED equivalence of units.** The `claude fable 5.1` claim at review line 195 accurately transcribes the available values. Provenance Scope calls `ts_parse_only__witness_status=broken_representation=flat__seed-0` a provider-outage case with “1 retry, 60s.” Its Cases row, also supplied as the complete ineligible-PASS excerpt, records:

| Field | Recorded value |
|---|---|
| `http_attempts_result` | 19 |
| `http_attempt_ceiling_result` | 240 |
| `elapsed_seconds` | 46.101 |
| `stage_statuses.provider_transport` | `completed_with_errors` |
| `status` / `verdict` | `scored` / `PASS` |
| `performance_eligible` | `False` |

Packet Table B identifies this as row 409. The full Cases row's `exclusion_reason` is “campaign report provider-outage case; final result explicitly attributes terminal failure to provider transient after bounded retries.” Its `final_notes` is empty and `final_metrics` supplies task labels, without a reconciliation of the attempt/time quantities.

The supplied records do not establish whether “1 retry” means the same unit as an HTTP attempt or whether “60s” denotes the measured elapsed duration. Therefore, retain an unresolved prose-versus-field contrast, not an asserted conversion or mechanism. These fields do not independently prove provider causation or authorize removing the recorded PASS.

## R4 — failure and guarantee breakdowns are available

**The stronger availability correction is supported.** In `grok 4.5`, review lines 40 and 65 accept unavailable environment-by-guarantee claims; line 63 says “Packet supplies globals” and softens the failure-breakdown correction to absence of a prebuilt table. Those conclusions understate the supplied packet. Packet Table B explicitly includes environment, verdict, guarantee, and both failure-label fields. Table D explicitly includes environment, `failure_modes`, and `judge_guarantees` (field descriptions at packet lines 57 and 567). An environment-level display table need not already exist for these recorded breakdowns to be available by aggregation.

The corresponding `claude fable 5.1` corrections at review lines 150–152 and 170–174 are supported. Cases produces exactly one guarantee per environment, with these totals:

| Guarantee | Environments | PASS | FAIL | Scored | Infrastructure-unscored |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `behavioral_reference` | 11 | 141 | 48 | 189 | 0 |
| `compile_only` | 27 | 136 | 80 | 216 | 11 |

The reviewer’s failure-breakdown examples also match the case rows: `source_invalid` is eight `monadic_reward` plus one `transformer_ssm_lift`; `rd_adaptive_halting` has five uppercase `RUNTIME_ERROR` FAILs; underfit counts include nine in each of the two zero-pass sheaf environments, six in `functorial_augmentation`, and four each in `gnn_message_passing` and `ssm_parallel_scan`.

Thus, remove evidence-unavailability claims from the factual handoff. Preserve the separate limit: guarantee is constant within each environment, so those counts do not supply a controlled within-environment comparison or a measured judge effect. Failure-label counts likewise do not identify mechanisms. Distinguish a missing preformatted display from missing source data.

No other review claims were audited in this note.

# Mercury POST-02 — Internal claim-check report (v3)

**Scope statement.** Retrospective editorial claim check on one archived campaign. No evaluation runs, model fits, causal estimates, cross-campaign imports, web searches, file opens, hash computations, code execution, or SVG renders were performed by this stage. All findings rest on the text supplied between the BEGIN/END markers in Parts 1–10. This report does not choose a prose winner, rewrite either draft, write the article, dispatch a stage, publish, or assert that the final writer, local validator, or human review has passed. Internal identifiers below (row numbers, labels, trace references) are for this report only and must not appear in public copy.

---

## 1. Input and stage status

### 1.1 Input manifest

| Recorded label / block | Source identifier | Scope received and read | Absence, truncation, access limitation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Governing instructions | CLAIM CHECK Parts 1–10 preamble | Complete, read | Quoted source text treated as data, not instruction |
| `gpt 6 luna max` (candidate) | `SOURCE WRITER_PAIR`, first response | Complete draft, frontmatter → closing figure block | Ends with a bare `!` line + "Figure 2" caption (transcript debris / forbidden figure) |
| `opus 5` (candidate) | `SOURCE WRITER_PAIR`, second response | Complete draft, frontmatter → "What this supports" | Split across Parts 1–2; reassembled contiguously |
| Evidence packet | `SOURCE COMPLETE_EVIDENCE_PACKET` | Table A (38 env rows), Table B (416 case rows), Table C (80 omission rows), Table D (340 matrix rows), Section E (Figures 8–11 + Fig 9 interval table) | Packet states free-text fields and case IDs omitted; questions needing those raw fields stay unresolved |
| Complete matrix CSV | `SOURCE COMPLETE_DIFFICULTY_MATRIX_CSV` | All 340 rows with full `difficulty_tuple` keys, `failure_modes`, `judge_guarantees` | None observed; row count reconciles with Table D |
| Provenance | `SOURCE CAMPAIGN_PROVENANCE` | Complete | Campaign source commit `ea827277…` (dirty=true) distinct from pinned analysis commit `6ed48cb1…`; hashes preserved as supplied, **not verified here** |
| Earlier overview | `SOURCE EARLIER_ANALYSIS_OVERVIEW` | Complete | Contains "10 environments at 0.00", "~12 at 1.00", proxy rates 0.30/0.25 — see §4 |
| Ineligible PASS row | `SOURCE INELIGIBLE_PASS_CSV_EXCERPT` | Header + single complete row | Single row only; no comparator rows supplied |
| Current internal article | `SOURCE CURRENT_INTERNAL_ARTICLE` | Complete | Exceeds the 2,000–2,800-word target; carries fig8–fig11 |
| House style | `SOURCE HOUSE_STYLE` | Complete | Style evidence only; not campaign evidence |
| Reference excerpts | `SOURCE ACTUAL_REFERENCE_POST_EXCERPTS` | Complete | Style evidence only; attribution text must not be imported |
| Prior-work references | `SOURCE PRIOR_WORK_REFERENCES` | Complete | Links **not** verified usable from this stage |
| Local source check | `SOURCE SUPPORTING_LOCAL_SOURCE_CHECK` (+ `.json`) | Complete, both | Attributed supporting local verification, **not** an Arena verdict; standalone `environment_summary.csv` absent from that snapshot (Table A supplies and agrees) |
| `grok 4.5` (v3 evidence review) | `SOURCE CURRENT_V3_EVIDENCE_REVIEW_PAIR`, first response | Complete | Asserts web access; recorded as asserted, not established |
| `claude fable 5.1` (v3 evidence review) | same block, second response | Complete | Asserts web access + repo landing page; recorded as asserted |
| `claude fable 5.1` (editorial blueprint) | `SOURCE CURRENT_EDITORIAL_BLUEPRINT_PAIR`, first response | Complete (§1–§7 + scope) | Cites an `ORCHESTRATION_CAPTURE_NOTE` **not supplied to this stage** |
| `grok 4.5` (editorial blueprint) | same block, second response | Complete | Same missing capture note; asserts "Web Search mode: Enabled per orchestration" |
| Review reconciliation | `SOURCE SUPPORTING_REVIEW_RECONCILIATION` | Complete (R1–R4) | Supporting local verification |
| Blueprint reconciliation | `SOURCE SUPPORTING_BLUEPRINT_RECONCILIATION` | Complete | Supporting orchestration findings, not an Arena verdict |
| SVG source check | `SOURCE SUPPORTING_SVG_SOURCE_CHECK` | Complete JSON | Source-geometry inspection only; `visual_rendering: NOT_PERFORMED` |

### 1.2 Stage status and recorded verdicts (as recorded, not endorsed)

| Response | Recorded self-verdict | Recorded external rating (per blueprint reconciliation) |
|---|---|---|
| `grok 4.5` review of `gpt 6 luna max` | accept with corrections — 0 blocking, 2 corrections, 7 notes | "Both are bad" (review pair) |
| `grok 4.5` review of `opus 5` | accept with corrections — 0 blocking, 3 corrections, 6 notes | " |
| `claude fable 5.1` review of `gpt 6 luna max` | accept with corrections — 1 blocking, 7 corrections, 1 note | " |

| `grok 4.5` blueprint | internal report, no verdict on candidates | " |

No older "accepted Stage C" label applies. "Accept with corrections" is a reviewer statement, not acceptance of either candidate. Two labelled responses do not establish reviewer independence or replication.

### 1.3 Dispatch, mode, and access

- **Arena Web Search mode:** the observed setting is **not present in the inputs received**. The orchestration capture note referenced by both blueprints was not supplied. `NOT_VERIFIABLE_FROM_RECEIVED_INPUTS`; `grok 4.5`'s blueprint assertion "Enabled per orchestration" is unsupported here (C-33).
- **External sources used by this stage:** none. No page was opened or read; no search was run.
- **Local paths:** printed in the prompt only. No local path was opened by this stage. Hashes supplied by the orchestrator (`88bc8fab…`, `23629f5b…`, `a028535f…`, `a4241adb…`, archive `ece98bcf…`, blob `b96d00d3…`, review file `2af1df65…`, four figure SHA-256 values) are **preserved as supplied and not verified**.
- **Handoff gaps (dependent conclusions marked unverified below):** (i) no observed Web Search setting; (ii) orchestration capture note absent; (iii) SVG renderings not inspected by anyone (source geometry only); (iv) raw free-text fields and case IDs omitted from the packet; (v) `PRIOR_WORK_REFERENCES` links unverified.
- Both current v3 reviews and both current blueprints **were** supplied; no required review or blueprint slot is empty.

### 1.4 Arithmetic re-derived here from the supplied rows

416 selected = 405 scored + 11 `infrastructure_unscored`; 405 = 277 PASS + 128 FAIL. 277 ÷ 405 = 0.683951 → **68.4%** (scored-case rate, unit: share of scored cases). 277 ÷ 416 = 0.665865 → **66.6%** (share of selected cases with an observed PASS). Environment groups: 17 all-pass (172/172), 7 scored zero-pass (0/68), 12 mixed (105/165 + 1 unscored), 2 unscored-only (10 unscored); 172 + 68 + 165 = 405; 172 + 105 = 277. Group shares of scored cases: 172/405 = 42.47%, 68/405 = 16.79%, 165/405 = 40.74%. Endpoints: 17 + 7 = 24 of 36 scored environments. Unweighted mean of 36 scored-environment fractions = 69.208%; median = positions 18–19 = 0.889 = 8/9. Matrix: 340 rows = 296 single-case + 44 multi-case (`moco` 15/55, `glyph` 12/24, `css_state_machine` 9/18, `epistemic_games` 8/23); 329 rated = 320 endpoint + 9 fractional (all `moco`); 320/329 = 97.26%. Uniform rows: all-pass env rows 148 + zero-pass env rows 56 = **204** (204/340 = 60.0%); + `epistemic_silence` 6 + `epistemic_type_games` 4 = **214** (62.94%). Zero-pass tuples 98 across 19 environments (sum re-derived by environment: 13+2+2+9+12+4+1+9+6+4+9+9+1+1+4+3+1+4+4 = 98). `moco` rows: 9 fractional (values 2/3 ×4, 5/6, 1/6, 3/4, 2/5, 1/3), 6 endpoint; passes 36 of 55 cases. Distinct difficulty-key names counted across the complete matrix = **34**. Labels: 51 + 49 + (9 `RUNTIME_ERROR` + 2 `runtime_error` = 11) + 9 + 5 + 3 = 128. Guarantees: 11 BR environments / 189 cases, 141 PASS + 48 FAIL; 27 CO environments / 227 cases, 136 PASS + 80 FAIL + 11 unscored; 141 + 136 = 277; 141/189 = 74.60%; 136/216 = 62.96%. Dispositions: 10 `rejected` FAILs (8 `monadic_reward` + 1 `batchnorm_ema` + 1 `transformer_ssm_lift`; 9 `source_invalid` + 1 `training_failed`); 9 FAILs with status `scored` and disposition `unscored` (5 `invalid_action`, 2 of the 3 `training_failed`, both lowercase `runtime_error`). Omissions: 56 + 14 + 10 = 80; by environment `moco` 35, `glyph` 21 (9 gate + 12 twin), `rope` 15 (10 modality + 5 gate), `epistemic_games` 9; 416 + 80 = 496. Sensitivities: (277−36)/(405−55) = 241/350 = 68.857% (Δ +0.46 pp); 277/381 = 72.703% (Δ +4.31 pp); 241/326 = 73.926%. Environments within 5 percentage points of 68.4%: `moco` 65.5%, `tensor_functor` 66.7% → 2 of 36. 55/405 = 13.58%.

---

## 2. Claim decision table

Format per entry: **quotation + location** → source citation (+ arithmetic) → current review finding → blueprint proposal → **verdict** → **action** + supported replacement → local caveat/placement → all affected locations. "Evidence correction" vs "editorial preference" marked.

### Cluster A — Denominators, rates, outcome status

**C-01 — pooled rate.** `gpt 6 luna max`, opening: "Mercury-2.5 received PASS on **68.4% of 405 scored cases** in this archived campaign." `opus 5`, "First, count…": "So **277/405 = 68.4%** is the scored-case pass rate." Current article, same section: "So **277/405, or 68.4%**, is the scored-case pass rate (the case-weighted pooled rate)." *Source:* Table A column sums; Table B statuses; 277 ÷ 405 = 0.683951 (share of scored cases). *Reviews:* both confirm. *Blueprints:* both retain. **SUPPORTED → KEEP.** Caveat beside first use: these are recorded judge verdicts on selected cases, not repeated measurements of capability. Locations: both drafts' openings and conclusions; article opening, counting section, conclusion; title. Evidence correction: n/a.

**C-02 — all-selected share.** `gpt 6 luna max`: "**277/416, or 66.6%**, answers a different question… It must not be read as a pass rate that treats the unscored cases as failures." `opus 5`: "It should never be presented as a pass rate, because that would silently convert 11 unknown outcomes into failures." *Source:* 277 ÷ 416 = 0.665865. **SUPPORTED → KEEP** in both drafts and the article. Caveat is already adjacent; keep it adjacent.

**C-03 — unscored cause.** `gpt 6 luna max`: "Another 11 are unscored because of a judge-coverage gap; they are not a third outcome." `opus 5`: "Eleven cases were never scored — a judge-coverage gap, not a third outcome"; and "Being unscored is a property of the judging pipeline, not of the case's outcome." *Source:* Table B rows 109–118, 243 — `status = infrastructure_unscored`, blank verdict, `failure_mode_normalized = unknown`; no cause field. Current article already correct: "The archive labels them `infrastructure_unscored` and records no further cause, so I do not describe them as judge gaps." *Reviews:* `claude fable 5.1` correction (both drafts); `grok 4.5` silent. *Blueprints:* both require the recorded label. **CORRECTION_REQUIRED → NARROW** to: "11 cases carry the recorded status `infrastructure_unscored`; the records state no cause." Locations: `gpt` counting section + "Unscored cases are not failures"; `opus` counting section + "Unscored cases are not failures" (2 sentences). Evidence correction.

**C-04 — four-way environment table.** Both drafts and the article: 17 / 7 / 12 / 2 with 172/172, 0/68, 105/165, 10 unscored and shares 42.5 / 16.8 / 40.7%. *Source:* Table A; arithmetic in §1.4. **SUPPORTED → KEEP.** Caveat: "uniform outcome" is not stability; counts rest on 4–24 cases each.

## 5. Permitted factual content

**5.1 Descriptive, supportable with stated units and scope (public-safe as written, each still needing its scope words "on this archived selection"):** 416 selected / 405 scored / 11 `infrastructure_unscored`; 277 PASS / 128 FAIL; 277/405 = 68.4% of scored cases; 277/416 = 66.6% of selected cases with an observed PASS; 17 / 7 / 12 / 2 environment groups with 172/172, 0/68, 105/165 (+1 unscored), 10 unscored; 24 of 36 scored environments at an endpoint; group shares 42.5 / 16.8 / 40.7%; unweighted mean 69.2% and median 8/9 across the 36 scored environments; all per-environment fractions in Table A; `glyph` + `moco` + `batchnorm_ema` = 94 of 405 scored cases and 56 of 128 FAILs; only `moco` (65.5%) and `tensor_functor` (66.7%) within 5 percentage points of 68.4%; 340 rows, 4–15 per environment, six above 10; 296 single-case (285 scored + 11 unscored) and 44 multi-case in four environments; 329 rated, 320 (97.3%) at 0 or 1, 9 fractional all in `moco` (values 1/6, 1/3, 2/5, 2/3 ×4, 3/4, 5/6; 6 endpoint rows; 36/55 overall); 34 environment-specific difficulty-key names recorded for every row; 204 uniform-scored-outcome rows + 10 rows in two entirely unscored environments; 98 zero-pass tuples in 19 environments, including all 12 mixed environments; 11 unscored cases = 6 `epistemic_silence` + 4 `epistemic_type_games` + 1 `rd_adaptive_halting` (all-easy five-dimension row); label totals 51/49/11/9/5/3 = 128 and the per-environment breakdown; 189 BR / 227 CO cases, 11 BR / 27 CO environments, 141/189 and 136/216 scored, 11 unscored all CO; 10 `rejected` and 9 conflicting-disposition FAILs with their environments and labels; omissions 56/14/10 = 80 with environment split 35/21/15/9 and 496 recorded case IDs; `css_state_machine` nine rows all 2/2; the single `sql_fixed_point` FAIL at `hidden_depth=hard, surface_deceptiveness=easy`; sensitivity arithmetic 68.86 / 72.70 / 73.93%; seed 0 for every supplied selected record; `dirty=true` and 0 config hashes checked.

**5.2 Supportable only with an adjacent qualification (correct arithmetic is not sufficient):** the pooled 68.4% (judged verdicts on a selection, one seed); every endpoint (4–24 cases, one seed); Wilson intervals (binomial sampling convention, not seed variation; selection and possible dependence); the guarantee contrast 141/189 vs 136/216 (confounded with environment; one guarantee per environment); label counts (recorded classifications, not mechanisms); the uniform-row figures (shared absence of scored outcomes is not an outcome); the moco keys (description only; no dimension effect, trend, scale, or ranking); 9/9 environments (4 BR + 5 CO; a compile-only PASS is not behavioral validation); the `ts_parse_only` PASS (recorded exclusion wording conflicts with the verdict); the `rejected` and conflicting-disposition cases (undefined; qualify affected comparisons); sensitivity arithmetic (arithmetic on recorded rows, and the `moco`-shrink row is an explicit hypothetical); selection concentration (descriptive; no causal effect of omission); 23/23 for `epistemic_games` (Bayesian inference over stipulated policies if described at all); the 496 frame ("design cells" is a chart label, not a unit, and is not the 340 rows).

**5.3 To remove or never introduce:** "no row-by-row key / cannot map `moco`" (C-18); "no environment-by-label / environment-by-guarantee breakdown available" (C-25); "judge-coverage gap" / "property of the judging pipeline" (C-03); the quoted "one seed per cell" (C-08); any definition of "cell" (C-09); "the 24 cannot coexist with the selection frame" (C-13); "casing is a data-quality artefact" (C-29); "unrolled into cases" (C-44); "selection was not random" (C-42); any Figure 2 or non-contract figure path and the `!` debris (C-36); any seed-variance, correlation, independence, monotonic-trend, ranking, cross-environment scale, mechanism, understanding, or general-capability claim; the overview's proxy rates and MoCo transition pattern (C-46); Atria or other-campaign numbers; "recursive theory of mind"; any claim of visual figure inspection, hash verification, page access, or executed code; the four excluded-wording passages (C-47).

**6.1 Thesis constraints (source-supported).** The article may assert: 277/405 = 68.4% is the exact answer to one counting question on this archived selection, and the recorded structure beneath it is endpoint-heavy (24 of 36 scored environments at 0 or 1; only two within 5 percentage points of the pooled value), unevenly dense (three environments supply 94 of 405 scored cases and 56 of 128 FAILs), and mostly single-case at the tuple level (296 of 340 rows). **6.3 Exact numerical requirements.** Use only the figures in §5.1 with the units stated there. Required arithmetic statements, if given: 277 ÷ 405 = 68.4% (share of scored cases); 277 ÷ 416 = 66.6% (share of selected cases with an observed PASS); 141 ÷ 189 = 74.6% and 136 ÷ 216 = 63.0% (shares of scored cases within each guarantee group); 204 + 10 = 214 matrix rows; 56 + 14 + 10 = 80 omitted and 416 + 80 = 496 recorded case IDs; 320 ÷ 329 = 97.3% of rated rows at an endpoint. Do not cite another writer's rounded answer in place of the calculation.

**6.4 Caption requirements.** Fig 8: three stacked bars; 496 = 416 + 80 (56/14/10, the ten modality cases in `rope`); 416 = 405 + 11 `infrastructure_unscored` — the 11-case segment carries no numeric label in the image, so the caption supplies the count; 405 = 277 + 128; omitted cases enter no pass rate; the nine conflicting-disposition FAILs are **not** plotted; "design cells" is the chart's label, not a unit. Fig 9: one dot per environment with scored cases (36; the two unscored-only environments are not drawn); 95% Wilson bars under a binomial sampling convention — not measured seed variation; dotted pooled line at 277/405 = 68.4%; labels are k/n; three colour classes (no scored pass, mixed, all scored passed); selection and possible dependence constrain interpretation. Fig 10: 340 squares grouped by environment; colour is the row pass rate; grey marks the 11 rows with no scored case (distinct from the ten rows of the two entirely unscored environments); numbers mark the 44 multi-case rows; the 9 fractional rows are all `moco`; columns are not comparable across environments. Fig 11: global labels for all 128 FAILs (51/49/11/9/5/3), the 11 consolidating two recorded spellings as an analysis convention; no environment split in the image — the breakdown is the adjacent table. No caption may claim visual inspection.

**6.5 Source and judge limitations to state at their anchors.** Selected-case denominator and the 11 unknown outcomes (counting section); seed 0 across supplied selected records with no second seed (first endpoint or interval, plus Fig 9 caption — once in full, thereafter a clause); selection concentration in four environments, non-randomness not established (omissions table); guarantee fixed per environment, so the 74.6% vs 63.0% contrast is confounded (guarantee table); compile-only PASS is not behavioral validation (same place); labels are classifications, not mechanisms (labels section); undefined dispositions (mixed table and `monadic_reward`/`transformer_ssm_lift`/`batchnorm_ema` lines); recorded exclusion wording vs PASS verdict (beside `ts_parse_only` 4/4); dirty source and no clean config-hash comparison (beside any reproduction sentence, with `[m-word]` preserved). No ending disclaimer dump.

**6.6 Required withdrawals and open claims (may not be cut for length).** Withdrawn: the `css_state_machine` variation claim (nine rows all 2/2). **6.8 Full current site contract.** Frontmatter exactly `title: "What a 68.4% Pass Rate Hides: Mercury-2.5 Across 38 Environments"`, `date: 2026-10-16`, `layout: post`; no additional Jekyll fields. ### Figure decision and source limits

The supplied Figure 8–11 descriptions and supporting SVG check permit these caption facts; they do **not** authorize a claim of visual inspection:

- **Figure 8:** Three stacked bars represent 496 labelled “design cells” = 416 selected + 80 omitted; 416 = 405 scored + 11 unscored; 405 = 277 PASS + 128 FAIL. The 11-case segment has no numerical image label; the caption must supply 11. The nine disposition-conflict FAILs are not plotted as a separate segment. “Design cells” is a figure label, not a technical definition of a unit. Source: EP Section E, Figure 8; SVG check JSON, Figure 8 `stacked_bars`, `eleven_numeric_label`, and `disposition_conflicts`.
- **Figure 9:** 36 environments with scored cases; dot = PASS/scored, bars = 95% Wilson intervals under a binomial sampling convention, labels = k/n, dotted reference = pooled 68.4%, and color groups = no scored pass / mixed / all scored passed. The two no-scored environments are not drawn. Intervals are not seed-variation measurements; selection and possible dependence constrain interpretation. Source: EP Section E, Figure 9; SVG check JSON confirms labels and groups, with only two Wilson endpoint spot-checks.
- **Figure 10:** 340 environment-by-tuple squares, 11 grey unscored rows, 44 multi-case rows with numbers, and nine fractional rows, all in `moco`; columns are not comparable across environments. The ten rows in wholly unscored environments are not the same object as the 11 unscored matrix rows. Source: EP Section E, Figure 10; SVG check JSON `data_glyphs`, `unscored_glyphs`, and `multicase_numbers`.
- **Figure 11:** Six global failure-label bars for all 128 FAILs, with no environment split. The article must supply an accurate per-environment breakdown separately. Source: EP Section E, Figure 11; SVG check JSON `bars`, `labels_and_counts`, and `aggregation`.

## 3. Review and blueprint reconciliation

### Current v3 reviews as proposed findings

| Response / proposal | Reconciliation against source | Disposition |

### Current editorial blueprints as proposed findings

- **`claude fable 5.1` blueprint:** Most of its source-directed corrections are confirmed, including the 204 + 10 distinction, full `moco` keys, available cross-tabs, the nine disposition conflicts, exact figure requirements, and open historical discrepancies. Its proposed inference that a “planning unit is a row rather than a case” must be removed; the records do not define a technical cell unit. Its “2,000–2,800 words, excluding tables” changes the contract, which does not exclude tables.
- **`grok 4.5` blueprint:** Its core count and correction checklist is broadly consistent with the rows. Its proposal that a “cell approximately equals environment-by-tuple row by arithmetic inference” is unsupported and must be removed. Its statement “Arena Web Search mode: Enabled per orchestration” is **NOT VERIFIABLE** from the supplied material: no observed-setting record or orchestration manifest is present. The blueprint may not promote this to a verified stage condition.
- Both blueprints correctly preserve many unresolved issues and propose the required figures, but neither is an evidence verdict. Supporting materials report that both were rated “Both are bad”; because the underlying orchestration record is absent, that rating remains secondhand.
- **Scoped handoff disposition:** `claude fable 5.1` — **READY WITH SPECIFIED CORRECTIONS** for evidence use; correct the selection/randomness and four-environment claims, remove the cell-unit inference, preserve the contract’s word-count scope, and apply the source corrections above. `grok 4.5` — **READY WITH SPECIFIED CORRECTIONS** for evidence use; remove the cell-unit inference and treat Arena mode/path-manifest status as unverified. Neither blueprint is accepted as-is, and neither is a prose winner.

## 5. Permitted factual content

### Supported descriptive claims

These may be used with the units shown:

- **Selected/scored outcomes:** 416 selected cases; 405 scored (277 PASS, 128 FAIL); 11 `infrastructure_unscored`. `277/405 = 68.4%` scored-case PASS rate; `277/416 = 66.6%` selected-case share with an observed PASS.
- **Environment outcomes:** 17 all-pass environments (172/172), seven scored zero-pass (0/68), 12 mixed (105/165 PASS/scored, plus one unscored), and two unscored-only (ten unscored). These sum to 38 environments; 24 of the 36 with scored cases are at an observed endpoint.
- **Environment summaries:** the unweighted mean of 36 environment-specific scored pass fractions is 69.208%; the median is 8/9 = 88.889%. Label these as unweighted environment summaries, not case-weighted rates.
- **Density examples:** `glyph`, `moco`, and `batchnorm_ema` account for 94/405 scored cases and 56/128 FAILs. Exclusion sensitivities are arithmetic illustrations, not alternative observed results.
- **Matrix:** 340 rows; 296 single-case and 44 multi-case rows; 329 rated, 320 at 0 or 1, nine fractional, all in `moco`; 98 scored zero-pass tuple rows in 19 environments. The 34 difficulty-key names are environment-specific. `moco`’s full row keys are available.
- **Omissions:** 80 distinct omitted case IDs: 56 intervention-twin, 14 detailed known-gate, and ten unsupported-modality rows. The ten modality rows are in `rope`. Environment totals are `moco` 35, `glyph` 21, `rope` 15, and `epistemic_games` 9.
- **Recorded failure labels:** 51 `underfit`, 49 `overfit_visible_tests`, 11 normalized runtime errors, nine `source_invalid`, five `invalid_action`, and three `training_failed`; the normalized total is 128. The raw runtime spellings and disposition association must be disclosed if discussed.
- **Recorded judge guarantees:** 11 `behavioral_reference` environments and 27 `compile_only`; scored results are 141/189 and 136/216, with all 11 unscored cases in the compile-only group. This is descriptive, not a measured judge effect.

### Claims that need an adjacent qualification

- All rates are recorded judgments for this selected, archived campaign, not general capability or understanding.
- The supplied selected-case records show seed 0; no repeated-seed data are supplied. This is not a variance estimate or an archive-wide claim.
- A compile-only PASS is not behavioral validation. Even a behavioral-reference PASS only describes what that case’s recorded judge checked.
- The Wilson intervals use a binomial sampling convention, not measured seed variation; selection and possible dependence constrain interpretation.
- Disposition flags and the ineligible PASS must remain visible beside comparisons they affect.
- Dirty source, missing clean config-hash comparison, omissions, and the unresolved historical counts constrain reproduction and interpretation.
- Tuple keys enable a descriptive map only; they do not establish isolated effects, monotonicity, a shared scale, or ranking.

### Remove or do not introduce

- “No `moco` tuple keys,” “no environment-by-label breakdown,” or “no environment-by-guarantee breakdown.”
- A claim that 214 rows are 214 observed success/failure outcomes without explicitly including the ten wholly unscored rows’ shared absence of outcomes.
- A technical definition of “cell,” or a claim that the arithmetic forces an environment-by-tuple sampling unit.
- Assertions of independence, correlation, seed variance, causal effects, mechanisms, difficulty effects, rankings, or stable endpoints.
- “Selection was random” or “selection was not random.”
- A reconciliation of 14 versus 24, seven versus 10, or 17 versus approximately 12.
- A claim that the provider note proves cause or permits changing row 409’s PASS.
- Figure 2 or any unverified figure content.
- Task details not supported by supplied task evidence, cross-campaign numbers, new analyses, fitted models, or new runs.

## 6. Precise final-writer handoff

### Evidence and section-level requirements

1. **Thesis and opening:** Keep 68.4% as the precise 277/405 scored-case rate. Distinguish it immediately from 277/416 observed-PASS share. Frame the environment and tuple maps as descriptive records, not replacements for the pooled figure or estimates of capability.
2. **Counts and selection frame:** Keep 416, 405, 277, 128, and 11 distinct. If using the 496 label from Figure 8, do not define “design cells” as a technical unit. Show 80 omissions and the 14-versus-24 conflict beside the frame. No random/non-random selection claim.
3. **Environment map:** Use 17/7/12/2, 172/68/165 scored cases, and the mixed table only with the local disposition flags. Keep the nine `ts_parse_only` PASSes’ four-case environment result alongside the row-409 eligibility/provider-note conflict. Keep `monadic_reward` 0/9, `transformer_ssm_lift` 8/9, and `batchnorm_ema` 2/15 qualified by the relevant dispositions.
4. **Density and names:** The 94/405 and 56/128 arithmetic is supportable, but is descriptive. If using exclusion sensitivities, label them arithmetic only and say they reflect selected case density, not an undocumented “unrolling” mechanism. Related names do not establish common outcomes, conditions, or causes.
5. **Tuple layer:** Use 340, 296/44, 329/320/9, and 98 tuples in 19 environments with units. Replace 214 with 204 uniform-scored rows plus ten rows in wholly unscored environments; if 214 is retained, state the shared absence of scored outcomes. Use the available keys descriptively; no effect, ranking, common scale, or cell-unit inference. State the all-easy `rd_adaptive_halting` tuple has no scored outcome.
6. **Withdrawals/open claims:** State the `css_state_machine` withdrawal: nine rows, each 2/2. Keep `moco` and `sql_fixed_point` as open questions only; name the SQL tuple (`hidden_depth=hard`, `surface_deceptiveness=easy`) if discussed, and say one observation establishes no effect. Preserve the 14/24, seven/10, and 17/approximately-12 discrepancies.
7. **Failure labels and judge limits:** Figure 11 is global only. If the article says an environment breakdown is present, provide a readable row-level table. The following cross-tab is supported by EP Table B/DM and the local reconciliation; raw runtime casing is retained:
